[[Backstories]] define the skills and restrictions of the [[colonist]] in question and may either enhance or impair the colonist's competence regarding specific [[skills]].

It was once possible to buy a [https://www.kickstarter.com/projects/tynansylvester/rimworld/rewards Kickstarter reward] that allowed the addition of a character with a specific name and backstory into the game. While this is no longer possible, further additions to the player-created pawn list are occasionally awarded for merit to the community, such as to honor especially prolific mod authors or content creators. Additionally, a higher-tier Kickstarter reward was "Pirate King", which allowed the pawn to be a faction leader. There are currently 308 of these player-created characters, 9 of which are designated as "rare". Rare pawns were created by developers, have a 50% lower spawn rate compared to the others, and cannot be added to the player's "Preferred Name in Game characters" list.

Backers were instructed to follow [https://docs.google.com/document/d/1HmLwOYk8e_kyQ4nEEfHtLr-vvPb03vkaOntnrVlPKM0/pub a set of guidelines] when creating the backstories to ensure that they fit in the pre-established RimWorld universe.
